| es-rental-income-0004 / LF23 | ## Who this applies to | [LIRPF art. 22](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a22) covers an individual's income from letting/ceding property or real enjoyment rights outside an economic activity. | S0 art.22; S0 art.25 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a22; https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a25 | sources/S0.reader.txt LF2421: “todos los que se deriven del arrendamiento o de la constitución o cesión de derechos o facultades de uso o disfrute sobre aquéllos, cualquiera que sea su denominación o naturaleza.”; sources/S0.reader.txt LF2423: “excluido el Impuesto sobre el Valor Añadido o, en su caso, el Impuesto General Indirecto Canario.”; sources/S0.reader.txt LF2723: “subarrendamiento percibidos por el subarrendador, que no constituyan actividades económicas.”; sources/S0.reader.txt LF2723: “arrendamiento de bienes muebles, negocios o minas, así como los procedentes del subarrendamiento percibidos por el subarrendador, que no constituyan actividades económicas.” | 2026-10-28; Express rule / named preserved statutory version / stated bounded administrative method |

| es-rental-income-0059 / LF80 | ## The base / ### Property income, expenses and depreciation | Start from everything payable for the letting, including goods let with it and tenant payments of the landlord's obligations, excluding separately charged VAT/IGIC under [LIRPF art. 22.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a22). | S0 art.22; S0 art.14 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a22; https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a14 | sources/S0.reader.txt LF2421: “todos los que se deriven del arrendamiento o de la constitución o cesión de derechos o facultades de uso o disfrute sobre aquéllos, cualquiera que sea su denominación o naturaleza.”; sources/S0.reader.txt LF2423: “excluido el Impuesto sobre el Valor Añadido o, en su caso, el Impuesto General Indirecto Canario.”; sources/S0.reader.txt LF1851: “sean exigibles por su perceptor.” | 2026-10-28; Express rule / named preserved statutory version / stated bounded administrative method |

| es-rental-income-0099 / LF98 | ## The base / ### Apply relief to each positive property result | **Company tenant under preserved housing wording.** Where DT38 preserves the earlier art.23.2 wording, a company paying the rent does not by itself disqualify an individual landlord from housing relief. | es-rental-income-0149 / LF114 | ## The base / ### Partial use, indirect tax and annual handoff | For VAT, use [es-vat-return](es-vat-return.md). | S5 art.7 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2026-20823#a7 | sources/S5.reader.txt LF1866: “Con efectos desde el 1 de diciembre de 2026, se modifica el artículo 20.Uno.23.º, letra e´) que queda redactado de la siguiente forma:”; sources/S5.reader.txt LF1872: “no supere las 30 noches, excepto si se produce en el apartamento o vivienda arrendada en la que el arrendador tenga su residencia habitual, en cuyo caso estarán exentos.»”; sources/S5.reader.txt LF1870: “servicios complementarios propios de la industria hotelera, tales como los de restaurante, limpieza, lavado de ropa u otros análogos.” | 2026-10-28; Express rule / named preserved statutory version / stated bounded administrative method |
| es-rental-income-0150 / LF114 | ## The base / ### Partial use, indirect tax and annual handoff | The 2026 December amendment can remove exemption from the specified furnished stays of no more than thirty nights outside the landlord's main home, in addition to the hotel-service route. | S5 art.7 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2026-20823#a7 | sources/S5.reader.txt LF1866: “Con efectos desde el 1 de diciembre de 2026, se modifica el artículo 20.Uno.23.º, letra e´) que queda redactado de la siguiente forma:”; sources/S5.reader.txt LF1872: “no supere las 30 noches, excepto si se produce en el apartamento o vivienda arrendada en la que el arrendador tenga su residencia habitual, en cuyo caso estarán exentos.»”; sources/S5.reader.txt LF1870: “servicios complementarios propios de la industria hotelera, tales como los de restaurante, limpieza, lavado de ropa u otros análogos.” | 2026-10-28; Express rule / named preserved statutory version / stated bounded administrative method |
| es-rental-income-0151 / LF114 | ## The base / ### Partial use, indirect tax and annual handoff | It does not by itself turn every tourist let into an IRPF economic activity or permanent-home relief case ([RDL 29/2026 art. 7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2026-20823)). | S5 art.7 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2026-20823#a7 | sources/S5.reader.txt LF1866: “Con efectos desde el 1 de diciembre de 2026, se modifica el artículo 20.Uno.23.º, letra e´) que queda redactado de la siguiente forma:”; sources/S5.reader.txt LF1872: “no supere las 30 noches, excepto si se produce en el apartamento o vivienda arrendada en la que el arrendador tenga su residencia habitual, en cuyo caso estarán exentos.»”; sources/S5.reader.txt LF1870: “servicios complementarios propios de la industria hotelera, tales como los de restaurante, limpieza, lavado de ropa u otros análogos.” | 2026-10-28; Express rule / named preserved statutory version / stated bounded administrative method |
| es-rental-income-0152 / LF114 | ## The base / ### Partial use, indirect tax and annual handoff | Keep income-tax category, VAT and tourist licensing as separate tests. | S5 art.7 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2026-20823#a7 | sources/S5.reader.txt LF1866: “Con efectos desde el 1 de diciembre de 2026, se modifica el artículo 20.Uno.23.º, letra e´) que queda redactado de la siguiente forma:”; sources/S5.reader.txt LF1872: “no supere las 30 noches, excepto si se produce en el apartamento o vivienda arrendada en la que el arrendador tenga su residencia habitual, en cuyo caso estarán exentos.»”; sources/S5.reader.txt LF1870: “servicios complementarios propios de la industria hotelera, tales como los de restaurante, limpieza, lavado de ropa u otros análogos.” | 2026-10-28; Express rule / named preserved statutory version / stated bounded administrative method |
| es-rental-income-0153 / LF116 | ## The base / ### Partial use, indirect tax and annual handoff | Aggregate the computed property results and imputation with other **general** income, then run the verified state/community/minimum/credit procedure in [es-income-tax](es-income-tax.md). | S2 art.100 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a100 | sources/S2.reader.txt LF5956: “19 por ciento sobre todos los conceptos que se satisfagan al arrendador, excluido el Impuesto sobre el Valor Añadido.”; sources/S2.reader.txt LF5958: “60 por ciento cuando el inmueble urbano esté situado en Ceuta o Melilla, en los términos previstos en el artículo 68.4 de la Ley del Impuesto.” | 2026-10-28; Express rule / named preserved statutory version / stated bounded administrative method |
| es-rental-income-0154 / LF116 | ## The base / ### Partial use, indirect tax and annual handoff | A tenant's withholding is a tax prepayment, not a rental expense. | S2 art.100 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a100 | sources/S2.reader.txt LF5956: “19 por ciento sobre todos los conceptos que se satisfagan al arrendador, excluido el Impuesto sobre el Valor Añadido.”; sources/S2.reader.txt LF5958: “60 por ciento cuando el inmueble urbano esté situado en Ceuta o Melilla, en los términos previstos en el artículo 68.4 de la Ley del Impuesto.” | 2026-10-28; Express rule / named preserved statutory version / stated bounded administrative method |
| es-rental-income-0155 / LF116 | ## The base / ### Partial use, indirect tax and annual handoff | The resident urban-rent rate is ordinarily 19% on subject payments excluding VAT, with statutory payer/exemption conditions ([RIRPF arts. 75/76/100](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a100)) | S2 art.100 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a100 | sources/S2.reader.txt LF5956: “19 por ciento sobre todos los conceptos que se satisfagan al arrendador, excluido el Impuesto sobre el Valor Añadido.”; sources/S2.reader.txt LF5958: “60 por ciento cuando el inmueble urbano esté situado en Ceuta o Melilla, en los términos previstos en el artículo 68.4 de la Ley del Impuesto.” | 2026-10-28; Express rule / named preserved statutory version / stated bounded administrative method |
| es-rental-income-0156 / LF116 | ## The base / ### Partial use, indirect tax and annual handoff | do not apply professional Modelo 111 to it. | S2 art.100 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a100 | sources/S2.reader.txt LF5956: “19 por ciento sobre todos los conceptos que se satisfagan al arrendador, excluido el Impuesto sobre el Valor Añadido.”; sources/S2.reader.txt LF5958: “60 por ciento cuando el inmueble urbano esté situado en Ceuta o Melilla, en los términos previstos en el artículo 68.4 de la Ley del Impuesto.” | 2026-10-28; Express rule / named preserved statutory version / stated bounded administrative method |

| es-rental-income-0158 / LF120 | ## The base / ### Urban-rent withholding exceptions | After establishing that the payer must withhold under RIRPF art. 76, apply the art. 75.3.g exclusions: a company renting housing for its employees | S2 art.75; S2 art.76; S2 art.100 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a75; https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a76; https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a100 | sources/S2.reader.txt LF4276: “900 euros anuales.”; sources/S2.reader.txt LF4274: “empleados.”; sources/S2.reader.txt LF4278: “grupo 861 de la Sección Primera de las Tarifas del Impuesto sobre Actividades Económicas, aprobadas por el Real Decreto Legisla­tivo 1175/1990, de 28 de septiembre, o en algún otro epígrafe que faculte para la actividad de arrendamiento o subarrendamiento de bienes inmuebles urbanos, y aplicando”; sources/S2.reader.txt LF4374: “cuando satisfagan rentas en el ejercicio de sus actividades.”; sources/S2.reader.txt LF5956: “19 por ciento sobre todos los conceptos que se satisfagan al arrendador, excluido el Impuesto sobre el Valor Añadido.”; sources/S2.reader.txt LF5958: “60 por ciento cuando el inmueble urbano esté situado en Ceuta o Melilla, en los términos previstos en el artículo 68.4 de la Ley del Impuesto.” | 2026-10-28; Express rule / named preserved statutory version / stated bounded administrative method |

| es-rental-income-0160 / LF120 | ## The base / ### Urban-rent withholding exceptions | or a landlord in IAE group 861 (or another authorising letting/subletting heading) for whom applying group 861's rules to the rented properties' cadastral values produces a **nonzero quota**, evidenced to the tenant through the required certification. | S2 art.75; S2 art.76; S2 art.100 | https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a75; https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a76; https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a100 | sources/S2.reader.txt LF4276: “900 euros anuales.”; sources/S2.reader.txt LF4274: “empleados.”; sources/S2.reader.txt LF4278: “grupo 861 de la Sección Primera de las Tarifas del Impuesto sobre Actividades Económicas, aprobadas por el Real Decreto Legisla­tivo 1175/1990, de 28 de septiembre, o en algún otro epígrafe que faculte para la actividad de arrendamiento o subarrendamiento de bienes inmuebles urbanos, y aplicando”; sources/S2.reader.txt LF4374: “cuando satisfagan rentas en el ejercicio de sus actividades.”; sources/S2.reader.txt LF5956: “19 por ciento sobre todos los conceptos que se satisfagan al arrendador, excluido el Impuesto sobre el Valor Añadido.”; sources/S2.reader.txt LF5958: “60 por ciento cuando el inmueble urbano esté situado en Ceuta o Melilla, en los términos previstos en el artículo 68.4 de la Ley del Impuesto.” | 2026-10-28; Express rule / named preserved statutory version / stated bounded administrative method |

| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF1 | ---<br>name: es-rental-income<br>description: Use this skill whenever asked about Spanish rental income taxation. Trigger on phrases like "rental income Spain", "alquiler IRPF", "rendimientos del capital inmobiliario", "rental deductions Spain", "60% reduction rental Spain", "non-resident rental Spain", "IBI deduction", "Modelo 100 rental", "vivienda turística", "imputación de rentas inmobiliarias", "valor catastral", "amortización inmueble", or any question about declaring rental income in Spain. This skill covers IRPF rental computation, deductible expenses, the 60% reduction for residential rental, non-resident flat rate, tourist rental, and imputed income for vacant properties. ALWAYS read this skill before touching any Spanish rental income work.<br>version: "1.0"<br>jurisdiction: ES<br>tax_year: 2026<br>last_updated: 2026-09-23<br>review_status: pending_review<br>trust_label: Built by Michael Cutajar and the OpenAccountants team<br>drafted_by: OpenAccountants<br>approved_by: pending<br>category: international<br>tier: 2<br>license: AGPL-3.0-or-later (code) / OpenAccountants Guide License v1.0 (content)<br>--- | Current lease-date table preserves applicable historical60% population and distinguishes2026new ladder; no blanket current60%. |

| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF19 | Letting property in Spain is taxed as rendimientos del capital inmobiliario under articles 22 to 24 of Ley 35/2006, unless the letting amounts to an economic activity under article 27.2. A second home that is neither let nor the owner's main home produces imputed income under article 85. Figures are for tax year 2026. Two of the pages cited below carry a different date on their face, and each is named where it is used: the Agencia Tributaria practical income tax manual is the manual for 2025 (the return filed in 2026), and the Agencia Tributaria page on value added tax for tourist lets was last updated in 2023. The statute pages on boe.es are the consolidated texts in force in 2026. | Current supplied v1.3metadata/scope/H1; historical version/intro wording retained as history and changed by root before this ledger task, not silentlydeleted by thiswriter. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF21 | One thing to read before anything else: the reduction on letting a home is no longer a single 60 per cent. Ley 12/2023 replaced it with five different cases, each with its own condition, and left older contracts on the old rule. A percentage quoted without its condition is worse than no answer. Section 2.5 sets out all five. | Current supplied v1.3metadata/scope/H1; historical version/intro wording retained as history and changed by root before this ledger task, not silentlydeleted by thiswriter. |

| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF41 | ### IRPF General Tax Rates: the escala general, state half only | Presentation heading/table separator, not a separate substantive tax rule. | actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF43 | Rental income goes into the general taxable base and is taxed at the escala general, not at the savings rates. The scale below is the STATE half of that tax only. Article 74 of Ley 35/2006 leaves the other half to each autonomous community, and each community approves its own scale. No page on an allowed host publishes the seventeen community scales, so this Guide cannot state a total marginal rate for Spain. A rate quoted as "the Spanish rate" is a rate for a region nobody named. | Current Rates LF31: general-base routing preserved; complete state article63 table and regional half in linked es-income-tax.md, rather than treating state-half table as total rental tax. |

| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF91 | This question comes first, because it changes everything downstream: the income category, the expense rules, the withholding, the reduction, and for a non-resident whether there is a permanent establishment. | Current Who this applies LF23–27: full-time employee/economic activity and tourist-service classification. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF93 | Article 27.2 of Ley 35/2006 states the test in one sentence: letting property is carried on as an economic activity "únicamente cuando para la ordenación de esta se utilice, al menos, una persona empleada con contrato laboral y a jornada completa". So the test is an employed person, under a contract that labour law treats as an employment contract, and full time. There is no "local" or premises requirement in the current wording; that requirement was removed with effect from 1 January 2015. The number of properties does not enter the test, and neither does the amount of rent. | Current Who this applies LF23–27: full-time employee/economic activity and tourist-service classification. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF95 | The Agencia Tributaria adds two points on the same page. First, the requirement is not met by management work the owner does himself. Second, letting is also an economic activity where the owner does not merely put the property at the tenant's disposal but supplies services proper to the hotel trade (restaurant, cleaning during the stay, laundry and the like). | Current Who this applies LF23–27: full-time employee/economic activity and tourist-service classification. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF97 | If the letting is an economic activity, this Guide stops being the right one: the income is rendimientos de actividades económicas, computed under articles 27 to 32, and the reductions in article 23.2 do not apply. | Current Who this applies LF23–27: full-time employee/economic activity and tourist-service classification. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF99 | Sources for this section: the statute at https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764 and the Agencia Tributaria manual page at https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c04-rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario/rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario/concepto-rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario.html | Current Who this applies LF23–27: full-time employee/economic activity and tourist-service classification. |

The Agencia Tributaria page also records that the owner may deduct a lower figure, but when the property is later sold the amount deducted as depreciation must reduce the acquisition cost used to compute the capital gain. | Current The base LF88: acquisition/inherited costs, land allocation, asset/year/day/lifetime limits; furniture/depreciation table LF38–39. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF193 | - **There is a lifetime cap as well as a yearly one** Accumulated depreciation may not exceed the acquisition value of the building that produces the income, land excluded, whatever the yearly maximum allows. The Agencia Tributaria states it as "El límite de la amortización acumulada será el valor de adquisición del inmueble generador de los rendimientos", and the Tribunal Económico-Administrativo Central fixed it on 18 December 2025 (claim 00/00653/2025) as applying "con independencia del límite anual previsto en el artículo 14 del Reglamento". For a property bought for money the value is the one in article 35 of Ley 35/2006; for one inherited or given, the value in article 36. See https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c04-rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario/gastos-deducibles/cantidades-destinadas-amortizacion.html | Current The base LF88: acquisition/inherited costs, land allocation, asset/year/day/lifetime limits; furniture/depreciation table LF38–39. |

See section 6.1. | Current The base LF88: acquisition/inherited costs, land allocation, asset/year/day/lifetime limits; furniture/depreciation table LF38–39. |

| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF250 | - **Interest and repairs are capped together, per property** Article 23.1.a).1.º of Ley 35/2006 and article 13.a) of Real Decreto 439/2007 cap interest, other finance costs, and repair and maintenance costs TOGETHER at the gross income obtained from that property or right. They cannot create a loss by themselves. See https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764 | Current Rates LF37 and property formula LF86; interest+repairs cap and4year property-specific carry. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF251 | - **The excess carries forward four years, not five** The excess is deductible "en los cuatro años siguientes", and in each of those years the same cap applies, per property: the total for these same items may not exceed that year's gross income from that property. The earlier version of this Guide said five years. See https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820 | Current Rates LF37 and property formula LF86; interest+repairs cap and4year property-specific carry. |

| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF304 | **The two official sources do not agree, and neither covers 2026 cleanly.** The statute in article 85 gives the reduced rate where the revision took effect in the tax period or in the ten preceding tax periods. The Agencia Tributaria page gives the reduced rate where the revision took effect from 1 January 2012, but says that rule applies to 2023, 2024 and 2025 only. Behind that page sits disposición adicional quincuagésima quinta of Ley 35/2006, which is still headed "durante el período impositivo 2023": attempts to extend it to 2025 and beyond, by Real Decreto-ley 16/2025 and Real Decreto-ley 2/2026, were each left without effect when the Congress of Deputies repealed those decrees. Do not place a client on one reading. Compute both, state the difference, and get the date the cadastral value was revised from the property tax receipt or the cadastre. | Current imputation rowLF42 and Partial useLF112:2026 2%/DA55 1.1%, vacancy/under-construction/unusable exceptions;2027scale expressly separated. |

| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF429 | ### 6.1 Partially Rented Year | Presentation heading/table separator, not a separate substantive tax rule. Current Partial useLF112: rental/nonrental days, consistent expenses/depreciation and own-use allocation. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF431 | - **Partially rented year** Interest and other finance costs, insurance, community fees, the property tax, supplies and the rest belong to the number of days of the year the property was let. For the days it was not let, imputed income under section 2.8 applies, computed in proportion to those days. See https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c04-rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario/gastos-deducibles/cantidades-destinadas-amortizacion.html | Current Partial useLF112: rental/nonrental days, consistent expenses/depreciation and own-use allocation. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF432 | - **Successive or simultaneous uses** Where a property is let for part of the year and at the owner's disposal for the rest, the letting part is rendimiento del capital inmobiliario and the rest is imputed income. The same split applies where only part of the property is let. | Current Partial useLF112: rental/nonrental days, consistent expenses/depreciation and own-use allocation. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF433 | - **Waiting for a tenant is not the same as let** A property that is not let but is intended to be let (an inmueble en expectativa de alquiler) produces imputed income for that period, and no expense of that period is deductible, because no rental income arises in it. The Agencia Tributaria states it as "Las rentas procedentes de bienes inmuebles, que no se encuentran arrendados ni subarrendados, pero que están destinados a serlo (inmuebles en expectativa de alquiler), tributan como rentas imputadas y no cabe deducir gastos correspondientes a ese periodo", following the Tribunal Supremo judgment 270/2021 of 25 February. See https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c04-rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario/rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario/concepto-rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario.html | Current Partial useLF112: rental/nonrental days, consistent expenses/depreciation and own-use allocation. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF435 | ### 6.2 Below-Market Rent (e.g., to family) | Presentation heading/table separator, not a separate substantive tax rule. Current relief/cap section LF108 and operative2026art24: spouse/third-degree affinity relative minimum againstimputation, no future2027formula. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF437 | - **The statutory rule is about relatives, not about market rent** Article 24 of Ley 35/2006 says that where the tenant or sub-tenant is the taxpayer's spouse or a relative, including relatives by affinity, up to and including the third degree, the total net income may not be lower than the amount the rules of article 85 would give. That is the imputed income figure in section 2.8, not a market rent. The Agencia Tributaria applies it as a comparison made last: the reduced net income for that property is the greater of the net income after any reduction in section 2.5, and the minimum computable because of the kinship. See https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764 | Current relief/cap section LF108 and operative2026art24: spouse/third-degree affinity relative minimum againstimputation, no future2027formula. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF438 | - **What this Guide does not say** There is no general rule on an allowed page that the Agencia Tributaria imputes a market rent whenever rent is below market to an unrelated tenant. An earlier version of this Guide said so; it has been removed. Separate rules on estimated income and related-party transactions exist and are outside this Guide. | Current relief/cap section LF108 and operative2026art24: spouse/third-degree affinity relative minimum againstimputation, no future2027formula. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF440 | ### 6.3 Co-Ownership (Proindiviso) | Presentation heading/table separator, not a separate substantive tax rule. Current WhoLF23 and Partial useLF112: beneficial-right/share allocation and documented co-owner amounts. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF442 | - **Co-ownership** Each co-owner reports the share of income and expenses matching their share of ownership, and article 85.2 applies the same rule to imputed income. | Current WhoLF23 and Partial useLF112: beneficial-right/share allocation and documented co-owner amounts. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF444 | ### 6.4 Rental at a Loss | Presentation heading/table separator, not a separate substantive tax rule. Current signednet formula LF92–96 and example2 LF170–205: no reduction of negativeincome and separate cappedrepaircarry. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF446 | - **Rental at a loss** A negative net rental income is part of the general tax base and can be set against other general income under the offsetting rules. The reductions in section 2.5 do NOT apply to a loss: they apply only to positive net income. The Agencia Tributaria worked example makes the same point. | Current signednet formula LF92–96 and example2 LF170–205: no reduction of negativeincome and separate cappedrepaircarry. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF447 | - **What cannot create the loss** Interest, other finance costs and repairs cannot by themselves make the net income negative, because of the cap in section 2.6. A loss must come from the other expenses. | Current signednet formula LF92–96 and example2 LF170–205: no reduction of negativeincome and separate cappedrepaircarry. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF449 | ### 6.5 Non-Resident with Multiple Properties | Presentation heading/table separator, not a separate substantive tax rule. Current Scope/Deadlines/Cross-border and linked completeIRNRGuide: separatepropertyperiods/base/grouping conditions, not one blanketrentalrate. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF451 | - **Non-resident with multiple properties** A non-resident taxed without a permanent establishment is taxed separately for each accrual. Imputed income on an unlet property is also declared on modelo 210. Since accruals from 2024, rental income may be grouped ANNUALLY rather than quarterly, and the 2026 deadlines are in section 2.7. | Current Scope/Deadlines/Cross-border and linked completeIRNRGuide: separatepropertyperiods/base/grouping conditions, not one blanketrentalrate. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF453 | ## The method, step by step | Presentation heading/table separator, not a separate substantive tax rule. Current property-by-property The base LF76–120 and twooa cases LF138–205; annual general tax handedtoes-income-tax. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF455 | 1. Decide whether the letting is an economic activity. Apply the test in article 27.2 of Ley 35/2006 (at least one person employed under an employment contract and full time) and the hotel-services test on the Agencia Tributaria page at https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c04-rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario/rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario/concepto-rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario.html If either is met, stop: this is business income, not capital inmobiliario. | Current property-by-property The base LF76–120 and twooa cases LF138–205; annual general tax handedtoes-income-tax. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF456 | 2. Decide whether the owner is resident or non-resident. A non-resident without a permanent establishment is taxed under Real Decreto Legislativo 5/2004 on modelo 210, at the rates in section 2.7: https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2004-4527 | Current property-by-property The base LF76–120 and twooa cases LF138–205; annual general tax handedtoes-income-tax. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF457 | 3. Compute the gross income under article 22.2 of Ley 35/2006, value added tax excluded: https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764 | Current property-by-property The base LF76–120 and twooa cases LF138–205; annual general tax handedtoes-income-tax. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF458 | 4. Deduct the expenses in article 13 and depreciation under article 14 of Real Decreto 439/2007, apportioned to the days let, and apply the interest-and-repairs cap: https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820 | Current property-by-property The base LF76–120 and twooa cases LF138–205; annual general tax handedtoes-income-tax. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF459 | 5. Get the contract date. Before 26 May 2023, the old single reduction applies. On or after that date, work down the ladder in section 2.5 and prove the condition for the rung you pick: https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c04-rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario/reducciones-rendimiento-neto/arrendamiento-inmuebles-destinados-vivienda.html | Current property-by-property The base LF76–120 and twooa cases LF138–205; annual general tax handedtoes-income-tax. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF460 | 6. For any part of the year the property was not let, add imputed income under article 85 of Ley 35/2006, and record which of the two readings in section 2.8 you used and why: https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/vivienda-otros-inmuebles/imputacion-rentas-inmobiliarias/calculo-renta-imputada.html | Current property-by-property The base LF76–120 and twooa cases LF138–205; annual general tax handedtoes-income-tax. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF461 | 7. Check withholding. If the tenant is a business or professional, the rate in section 4A applies unless one of the three cases in article 75.3.g) of Real Decreto 439/2007 is met. The tenant files modelo 115: https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/irpf/retenciones-ingresos-cuenta-pagos-fraccionados/retenciones-ingresos-cuenta/modelo-115.html | Current property-by-property The base LF76–120 and twooa cases LF138–205; annual general tax handedtoes-income-tax. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF462 | 8. File. A resident declares on modelo 100 in the Renta campaign; a non-resident on modelo 210 within the deadlines in section 2.7: https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/todas-gestiones/impuestos-tasas/impuesto-sobre-renta-no-residentes/modelo-210-irnr______a-no-residentes-permanente_/nota-modificaciones-plazos-presentacion-modelo-210.html | Current property-by-property The base LF76–120 and twooa cases LF138–205; annual general tax handedtoes-income-tax. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF464 | ## Ask the client first | Current LF200, exact original unit |

This decides which reduction ladder applies, and it is the single most common error in Spanish rental tax. | Current sixquestionsLF209–214 preserve consequential residence,lease,use,expense,value and annualtax intake. |

Only a permanent home let opens the reductions in section 2.5. | Current sixquestionsLF209–214 preserve consequential residence,lease,use,expense,value and annualtax intake. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF468 | - Is the home in a stressed residential market area, and if so, is this a new contract with the rent cut, a first letting to a tenant aged between 18 and 35, or a let to a public body or qualifying non-profit? Each opens a different rung. | Current sixquestionsLF209–214 preserve consequential residence,lease,use,expense,value and annualtax intake. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF469 | - Do you employ anyone full time under an employment contract to run the lettings, or supply hotel-type services? | actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF470 | - Which autonomous community are you resident in? | actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF471 | - Do you have the property tax receipt showing the cadastral value split between land and construction, and the date the cadastral value was last revised? | actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF472 | - Is the tenant a business or a professional, and how much rent do they pay you in the year? | actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF504 | ## Section 7: Prohibitions | Presentation heading/table separator, not a separate substantive tax rule. Current The base/transition/positive-relief/proportionate-day and separateIRNR/VAT/withholding methods retain substantive prohibitions; no assumed exemptions or universalfine. |
| actual published main comparator (origin/main at start) 62b7639a5a68324a83a96bb0190fff8b7c76f8c5 LF506 | - **Prohibitions** NEVER quote a reduction percentage without the condition and the contract date that go with it; NEVER apply a reduction under article 23.2 to a tourist or seasonal let; NEVER apply a reduction under article 23.2 to a negative net income; NEVER apply a reduction under article 23.2 where the income was not in a self-assessment filed before a verification, limited review or inspection of that income began; NEVER let a non-resident outside the EU and EEA deduct expenses; NEVER depreciate the land component; NEVER exceed the building depreciation rate in section 2.4; NEVER deduct mortgage principal; NEVER let interest and repairs together exceed that property's gross income for the year; NEVER ignore imputed income for the days a non-main home was not let; NEVER state a total Spanish marginal rate without naming the autonomous community; NEVER present the imputation rate for 2026 as settled; NEVER present tax calculations as definitive, always label them as estimated. | Current The base/transition/positive-relief/proportionate-day and separateIRNR/VAT/withholding methods retain substantive prohibitions; no assumed exemptions or universalfine. |

| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF1 | ---<br>name: es-rental-income<br>description: Use this Guide to compute common-territory Spanish individual rental and own-use property income for 2026. It covers ownership and income classification, gross rent, property-specific interest and repair caps, other expenses, bad debts, depreciation and lifetime limits, residential net-income reductions, the October reform and December lease-date transition, relative lettings and imputed income. Trigger phrases include alquiler España, arrendamiento vivienda, rendimiento capital inmobiliario, reducción alquiler, amortización inmueble, gastos reparación, renta imputada and landlord tax Spain. Obtain actual lease and renewal dates, tenant use and age, prior rent and reference-price evidence, large-holder status, cadastral values, acquisition costs, rental days and prior expense ledgers. Resident rental results feed the full state and regional IRPF computation. Non-resident income, property businesses, corporate landlords and indirect-tax treatment have separate companion routes.<br>version: 1.3<br>jurisdiction: ES<br>tax_year: 2026<br>last_updated: 2026-10-28<br>review_status: pending_review<br>trust_label: Built by Michael Cutajar and the OpenAccountants team<br>drafted_by: OpenAccountants<br>approved_by: pending<br>category: international<br>tier: 2<br>license: AGPL-3.0-or-later (code) / OpenAccountants Guide License v1.0 (content)<br>--- | Current LF1, exact original unit |

| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF21 | ## Who this applies to | Current LF21, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF23 | [LIRPF art. 22](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a22) covers an individual's income from letting/ceding property or real enjoyment rights outside an economic activity. Identify the beneficial right, ownership share, use and lease dates. A sublessor's receipts and letting an operating business are **movable-capital income under art. 25.4.c** unless the activity rules apply; letting bare premises remains property income. The owner's rent from a tenant who sublets stays the owner's property income. Do not grant the property-income housing reduction to the sublessor's movable-capital receipts ([LIRPF art. 25.4.c](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a25)). | Current LF23, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF25 | Property letting is an economic activity under [art. 27.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a27) when its organisation uses at least one full-time employee under a genuine employment contract. The owner's own management does not satisfy that employment test. Hotel-type services can also change classification under the [AEAT explanation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c04-rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario/rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario/concepto-rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario.html). Do not decide income-tax or VAT classification from a tourist-registration label alone. | Current LF25, exact original unit |

Non-residents use [the IRNR Guide](es-non-resident-income-tax-irnr-and.md), including the 2026 revised filing periods and directly linked expense conditions. Foral taxpayers, corporate landlords, property economic activities and specialist indirect-tax territories need their applicable route. | Current LF27, exact original unit |

| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF78 | ### Property income, expenses and depreciation | Current LF78, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF80 | Start from everything payable for the letting, including goods let with it and tenant payments of the landlord's obligations, excluding separately charged VAT/IGIC under [LIRPF art. 22.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a22). Identify refundable deposits separately until their actual retention/application. Use [art. 14](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a14) accrual rules; a missing bank receipt does not automatically eliminate rent due. | Current LF80, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF82 | Under [RIRPF art. 13](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a13), deduct necessary supported interest/finance, repairs, non-penalty property taxes, community/management/caretaking, insurance, utilities, legal/formalisation costs and qualifying bad debts. Keep improvements/enlargement as capital, not repairs, and mortgage principal as capital repayment. A landlord's early-termination payment can be an improvement under the cited [AEAT classification](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c04-rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario/rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario/concepto-rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario.html); classify who pays whom and why. A previously deducted bad debt later collected returns to income in collection year. | Current LF82, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF84 | For the first ordinary doubtful-debt route, **concurso** under RIRPF13.e.1 is the specified insolvency-proceeding status; an unpaid balance or a debtor's financial difficulty alone is not proof of that status. Alternatively, under13.e.2, retain evidence of the landlord's **first collection action**, require **strictly more than six months** between it and the tax-period end, and verify no credit renewal. For a normal31December2026 year-end and no concurso, a first collection action on1July2026 does not satisfy that timing route; one on30June2026 does. With otherwise qualifying unpaid rent EUR1,000 already included in gross income, the latter facts support a EUR1,000 doubtful-debt expense; the1July facts alone support none under that route. The finance/repair gross-income cap does not extend to this separate expense. Later recovery of an amount actually deducted is income in the recovery year. The exceptional three-month rule for2020/2021 is not the2026 threshold ([RIRPF13.e](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a13)). | Current LF84, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF86 | For each property, let G be gross income, F current qualifying finance-plus-repair costs and C eligible earlier capped costs. Apply `capped_deduction = min(G, F +eligible_C)` in the statutory expiry/order schedule and preserve unused amounts by origin year. **Only this class is capped at gross income**. Other expenses and depreciation can create a negative net property result. Do not offset one property's unused repair limit against another property's rent. | Current LF86, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF88 | For the building, remove land from eligible acquisition value and cadastral value. If needed, allocate acquisition cost using the year's cadastral construction/land ratio. Annual maximum is `0.03 ×max(eligible_acquisition_building_value, cadastral_building_value)`, prorated for entitled rental days and ownership, and capped by **remaining depreciable acquisition value**. A higher cadastral basis does not create an unlimited lifetime deduction. For inherited/gift property use the supported art. 36/ISD acquisition value and costs, not only cash inheritance expenses. These annual/lifetime and inherited-value principles are explained in the [AEAT depreciation page](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c04-rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario/gastos-deducibles/cantidades-destinadas-amortizacion.html), including the identified Supreme Court/TEAC criteria. Retain the actual lawful depreciation schedule for later gain basis; do not double deduct an asset purchase. | Current LF88, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF90 | ### Apply relief to each positive property result | Current LF90, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF92 | `net_rent = gross_rent −capped_finance_repairs −other_allowed_expenses −allowed_depreciation`. | Current LF92, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF94 | If qualifying positive net rent, select the lease-date branch and reduction r: `housing_reduction = max(net_rent,0) ×r`; `taxable_net_rent = net_rent −housing_reduction`. A negative result is **not** multiplied by 40% or 50%. Apply reductions before aggregating different properties, so a positive property's relief does not shrink another property's loss. | Current LF94, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF96 | Housing relief applies only to the statutory permanent-home use and properly declared positive net income before a verification/review/inspection procedure begins. It does not cover omitted income/incorrect expenses regularised in that procedure, or contracts violating the specified LAU rent limitation ([art. 23.2](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a23)). Seasonal/tourist use does not become permanent housing merely because the building is residential. The identified corporate-tenant residential-use method is supplied below. Rehabilitation uses [RIRPF art. 41.1](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a41), including its structural and >25% value test, not ordinary repainting. | Current LF96, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF98 | **Company tenant under preserved housing wording.** Where DT38 preserves the earlier art.23.2 wording, a company paying the rent does not by itself disqualify an individual landlord from housing relief. [TEAC00/05138/2013,8September2016](https://serviciostelematicosext.hacienda.gob.es/TEAC/DYCTEA/criterio.aspx?id=00%2F05138%2F2013%2F00%2F0%2F1) establishes the route when the dwelling is proved to be the permanent home of identified natural persons. The [exact AEAT2025 housing explanation](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c04-rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario/reducciones-rendimiento-neto/arrendamiento-inmuebles-destinados-vivienda.html) requires the exclusive residential use of a particular natural person to be established from the outset; a generic contractual destination for the company's employees does not qualify under that explanation. Retain the lease, identification of the actual occupant and family where applicable, the residential-use terms and evidence of actual permanent occupation. The TEAC case recorded the named director/family, restrictions on changed use, and prohibitions on commercial use, assignment and subletting; those are evidence of the established purpose, not a new universal list of statutory clauses. | Current LF98, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF100 | Within that preserved population, choose the independently applicable preserved art.23.2/DT38 lease-date percentage above and apply it to eligible declared positive net income. Do not import the old50% percentage quoted in the2009 dispute into2026. Nor does an employee's age alone prove a higher-rate route whose actual statutory conditions concern the tenant. For a continuing lease signed1January2020, identified permanent-home use from the outset, all preserved60% conditions met, and12,000 net income after allowed expenses, housing relief is7,200 and reduced net4,800. An otherwise identical lease merely giving the company a generic right to house unspecified employees does not establish this relief; absent another applicable relief, reduced net remains12,000. A genuine seasonal stay also fails the permanent-home test. The new post-transition2026 art.23.2 expressly refers to LAUart.2.1, whereas TEAC's reasoning concerned earlier wording without that express incorporation. The supplied company-tenant conclusion therefore covers the preserved wording; the effect of the new reference on a post-transition company lease, including any company-tenant claim to the new-text80% tacit-extension route even where the original lease is older, remains specifically unconfirmed. No automatic entitlement or universal denial is asserted for that new population. These are the individual's IRPF housing rules; VAT exemption and the employer's benefit-in-kind treatment follow their separate companion methods ([currentLIRPF23/DT38](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a23); [AEAT housing-use boundary](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c04-rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario/reducciones-rendimiento-neto/arrendamiento-inmuebles-destinados-vivienda.html)). | Current LF100, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF102 | **Housing relief before irregular-income relief.** Test each entitlement independently. Under [LIRPF art.23.3](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a23), the 30% route requires generation over more than two years or the specific regulatory irregular-income category, with inclusion in one tax period. Ordinary monthly rent does not acquire that status because the lease lasts several years. [RIRPF art.15](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a15) lists the owner's business-lease transfer receipts, tenant/subtenant/assignee compensation for property damage, and lifetime use/enjoyment rights. A tenant's own lease-transfer proceeds instead follow the capital-gain route. Keep the specific pre-2015 instalment transition in LIRPF DT25.3 separate from a new receipt's single-period condition ([AEAT ordering and categories](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c04-rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario/reducciones-rendimiento-neto/rendimientos-periodo-generacion-superior-dos-anos.html)). | Current LF102, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF104 | For an amount independently qualifying for both routes, first calculate the applicable housing reduction on its eligible positive net income. Apply 30% only to the resulting qualifying irregular net amount, subject to the **300,000 annual irregular-income base ceiling**. The ceiling is tested after housing relief, not against the original rent. For one positive qualifying component and no competing irregular income, let `N` be its net income after expenses, `h` its independently established housing-reduction rate and `R=N×(1−h)`; the further reduction is `30%×min(R,300,000)`, and reduced income is `R` minus that amount. These conditions do not grant housing treatment to a commercial lease or make an otherwise regular receipt irregular. AEAT expressly applies this order; the current2026 law retains art.23.3 while the applicable housing percentage is selected under the actual art.23.2/DT38 lease route ([AEAT method](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c04-rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario/reducciones-rendimiento-neto/rendimientos-periodo-generacion-superior-dos-anos.html); [current law](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a23)). | Current LF104, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF106 | Hypothetical controls assume both entitlements have already been established and there are no other irregular amounts. With net10,000 and preserved housing relief60%, housing relief is6,000, leaving4,000; irregular relief is1,200 and taxable net2,800. The percentages are successive: adding60%+30% against the original10,000 would be wrong. If a separately qualifying current route gives90% housing relief, the same10,000 leaves1,000, then300 irregular relief and700 taxable net;100% housing relief leaves no further irregular base. Net800,000 with independently established50% housing relief leaves400,000: only300,000 receives30%, so irregular relief is90,000 and taxable net310,000. | Current LF106, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF108 | For several irregular amounts, retain their post-housing values and apply the annual ceiling across the taxpayer's claim. AEAT requires the algebraic sum of positive and negative irregular amounts; do not discard a negative amount before testing that ceiling. Where all the irregular amounts concerned are positive and their same-nature total exceeds the ceiling, distribute the maximum reduction proportionately. For post-housing amounts200,000 and400,000 with no other irregular amounts, the300,000 base splits100,000/200,000; relief is30,000/60,000, leaving170,000/340,000. These positive controls do not replace the signed treatment required for a negative irregular amount ([AEAT annual cap and apportionment](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/irpf-2025/c04-rendimientos-capital-inmobiliario/reducciones-rendimiento-neto/rendimientos-periodo-generacion-superior-dos-anos.html)). Keep a relative letting's [2026 art. 24](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764&p=20261002&tn=1#a24) minimum-result test, covering spouse/relatives through the third degree including affinity, against the relevant property imputation. The amended 2027 relative/imputation formula is not the 2026 rule. | Current LF108, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF110 | ### Partial use, indirect tax and annual handoff | Current LF110, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF112 | Allocate ownership, rental days and non-rental use consistently across income, deductible expenses and depreciation. For 2026 own-use income, use 2% or qualifying DA 55 1.1%, with unnotified-value and under-construction/unusable exceptions, as in [the cross-border property method](es-non-resident-income-tax-irnr-and.md#the-base). For a resident, include eligible imputation in the general base; a vacant investment property is not automatically the taxpayer's exempt main home. The future aggregate progressive imputation scale starts **2027**, not 2026. | Current LF112, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF114 | For VAT, use [es-vat-return](es-vat-return.md). The 2026 December amendment can remove exemption from the specified furnished stays of no more than thirty nights outside the landlord's main home, in addition to the hotel-service route. It does not by itself turn every tourist let into an IRPF economic activity or permanent-home relief case ([RDL 29/2026 art. 7](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2026-20823)). Keep income-tax category, VAT and tourist licensing as separate tests. | Current LF114, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF116 | Aggregate the computed property results and imputation with other **general** income, then run the verified state/community/minimum/credit procedure in [es-income-tax](es-income-tax.md). A tenant's withholding is a tax prepayment, not a rental expense. The resident urban-rent rate is ordinarily 19% on subject payments excluding VAT, with statutory payer/exemption conditions ([RIRPF arts. 75/76/100](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a100)); do not apply professional Modelo 111 to it. A non-resident uses the distinct IRNR rate/base/withholding and 210 route. | Current LF116, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF118 | ### Urban-rent withholding exceptions | Current LF118, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF120 | After establishing that the payer must withhold under RIRPF art. 76, apply the art. 75.3.g exclusions: a company renting housing for its employees; annual rent paid by the same tenant to the same landlord **not exceeding 900**; or a landlord in IAE group 861 (or another authorising letting/subletting heading) for whom applying group 861's rules to the rented properties' cadastral values produces a **nonzero quota**, evidenced to the tenant through the required certification. An IAE exemption or registration alone is not that certificate. A private tenant paying outside an economic activity does not acquire a withholding duty merely by paying more than 900. Where the urban property is in Ceuta/Melilla and meets LIRPF art. 68.4, art. 100 cuts the **19% rate by 60%**, giving a derived **7.6%**; it is not a 60-point cut ([RIRPF arts. 75.3.g/76/100](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a75)). | Current LF120, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF122 | ## Deadlines and filing | Current LF122, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF124 | Residents use Modelo 100 for the accrual year under the approved campaign, not assumed permanent dates for 2026 income filed in 2027. Preserve each property's lease, price history/index/zone evidence, beneficial ownership, IBI values, finance/repairs carry ledger, depreciation schedule and withholding certificate. | Current LF124, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF126 | For non-residents, 2026 own-use 210 has the new **1 April–31 December 2027** window, and payable annual rental grouping has **1–20 April 2027**. Ungrouped rental accruals through September 2026 retain the relevant quarterly route; from October the new following-April route applies. Nil/refund 210 rules and grouping conditions differ ([210 order art. 5](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2010-19707#a5); [2026 amendment/commencement](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2026-13573)). Refer to the complete [IRNR filing table](es-non-resident-income-tax-irnr-and.md#deadlines-and-filing) before selecting a period. Resident business-rent withholding is paid through Modelo 115 and reported annually on Modelo 180; use the [AEAT rent-withholding procedure](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/ayuda/manuales-videos-folletos/manuales-practicos/folleto-actividades-economicas/7-otras-obligaciones-fiscales-retenciones/7_3-retenciones-arrendamiento-bienes-inmuebles-180.html). | Current LF126, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF128 | The old tourist-intermediary **Modelo 179 ceased for 2024 and later years**. RD 117/2024's platform reporting replaces that real-estate information: **040** manages registration/change/deregistration in the platform-operator register, and **238** communicates reportable platform information under its operator/seller/property tests. These are operator obligations, not automatic landlord returns; a landlord still reconciles their own taxable income to platform statements ([AEAT 179 FAQ](https://sede.agenciatributaria.gob.es/Sede/todas-gestiones/impuestos-tasas/declaraciones-informativas/modelo-179-decla_____la-cesion-uso-turisticos/preguntas-frecuentes.html); [Orden HAC/72/2024](https://www.boe.es/buscar/doc.php?id=BOE-A-2024-2092)). | Current LF128, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF130 | ## Penalties and interest | Current LF130, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF132 | Use [LGT arts. 26–28/191/198](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2003-23186#a27): unprompted late tax-due filing generally has 1% plus 1% per complete month to twelve months, then 15% plus specified subsequent interest. Under-declaration, information failures and enforcement have their own conditions and amounts. A housing reduction can be unavailable on income/expense amounts found in an initiated check under art. 23.2; late payment interest is not a substitute for meeting the substantive relief conditions. Do not put an assumed universal landlord fine into the deductible expense schedule. | Current LF132, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF134 | ## Cross-border points | Current LF134, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF136 | A non-resident's Spanish-property rent uses the IRNR statutory gross or qualifying EU/EEA directly linked expense base and the residence-conditioned general rate, without automatic resident housing percentage reductions. A resident's foreign-property rent enters worldwide IRPF with the actual treaty/source/foreign-tax-credit analysis. A foreign account or agent does not establish tax residence or source. Preserve applicable treaty and foral allocation evidence; do not reduce Spanish tax by an unverified treaty assumption ([TRLIRNR arts. 13/24/25](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2004-4527#a24); [LIRPF arts. 2/80](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a80)). | Current LF136, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF138 | ## Two worked examples that recompute | Current LF138, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF140 | ### 1. Full-year preserved lease, depreciation and 50% reduction | Current LF140, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF142 | A hypothetical resident's permanent-home lease was signed in 2024 and remains within the preserved 2025 ladder, with no enhanced conditions. Gross 2026 rent is **12,000**. Qualifying interest/repairs are 2,000, other supported expenses 1,000. Total eligible acquisition value is 150,000, of which 80% is building; total cadastral value is 100,000 with the same construction share. It is rented all year, no accumulated cap is exhausted. Building depreciation at the chosen lawful 3% is `max(120,000,80,000) ×3%=3,600`. Net is **5,400**, 50% relief **2,700**, taxable general-base rental income **2,700**. Annual IRPF then depends on the person's full state/regional computation ([art. 23/DT 38](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2026-20823); [RIRPF art. 14](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2007-6820#a14)). | Current LF142, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF144 | ```oa-check<br>name: Spain 2026 preserved residential lease and depreciation<br>inputs:<br>  gross_rent: 12000<br>  finance_repairs: 2000<br>  other_expenses: 1000<br>  acquisition_total: 150000<br>  cadastral_total: 100000<br>  building_share: 0.80<br>steps:<br>  capped_expenses: min(gross_rent, finance_repairs)<br>  depreciation_base: max(acquisition_total * building_share, cadastral_total * building_share)<br>  depreciation: depreciation_base * 0.03<br>  net_rent: gross_rent - capped_expenses - other_expenses - depreciation<br>  housing_reduction: max(net_rent, 0) * 0.50<br>  taxable_net: net_rent - housing_reduction<br>expected:<br>  depreciation_base: 120000<br>  depreciation: 3600<br>  net_rent: 5400<br>  housing_reduction: 2700<br>  taxable_net: 2700<br>``` | Current LF144, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF168 | ### 2. Capped repair carryforward, unreduced loss and qualifying December first lease | Current LF168, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF170 | A hypothetical resident's property A earns **4,000**, with qualifying finance/repairs **6,000**, other expenses 800 and valid depreciation 1,200. Deduct only 4,000 finance/repairs; carry **2,000** in that property's four-year capped-cost ledger. Other expenses/depreciation give **−2,000 net**, with no housing reduction. | Current LF170, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF172 | The same owner acquires property B on **15 December 2026** and immediately makes its first permanent-home lease. Documentary facts establish that the landlord is not a large holder and the initial rent is **below** the legally applicable reference-price limit; all current art. 23.2.b.1 conditions hold. December gross rent is 1,200, with 200 properly computed allowable expenses/depreciation. Net **1,000** receives **100% reduction**, leaving zero taxable rental net. B has no own-use days during this ownership. Combined taxable rental result is **−2,000**, not a loss reduced by 50% or 100%. Other general income and annual loss/minimum rules are still required for final IRPF ([LIRPF art. 23](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2006-20764#a23); [RDL 29/2026 art. 6/DT 38](https://www.boe.es/buscar/act.php?id=BOE-A-2026-20823)). | Current LF172, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF174 | ```oa-check<br>name: Spain 2026 property-specific cap and December housing relief<br>inputs:<br>  a_rent: 4000<br>  a_finance_repairs: 6000<br>  a_other: 800<br>  a_depreciation: 1200<br>  b_rent: 1200<br>  b_allowed_expenses: 200<br>steps:<br>  a_capped_deduction: min(a_rent, a_finance_repairs)<br>  a_carryforward: a_finance_repairs - a_capped_deduction<br>  a_net: a_rent - a_capped_deduction - a_other - a_depreciation<br>  a_reduction: max(a_net, 0) * 0.50<br>  b_net: b_rent - b_allowed_expenses<br>  b_reduction: max(b_net, 0) * 1.00<br>  total_taxable_rental: a_net - a_reduction + b_net - b_reduction<br>expected:<br>  a_carryforward: 2000<br>  a_net: -2000<br>  a_reduction: 0<br>  b_net: 1000<br>  b_reduction: 1000<br>  total_taxable_rental: -2000<br>``` | Current LF174, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF200 | ## Ask the client first | Current LF200, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF202 | 1. What residence/territory, beneficial ownership and actual rental/business use applies to each property? | Current LF202, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF203 | 2. What lease/renewal dates, earlier uprated rent, tenant ages/use, large-holder status and official price/zone evidence establish the correct relief? | Current LF203, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF204 | 3. What gross payable rent, refunds/deposits and withholding reconcile to the lease, payment and accrual records? | Current LF204, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF205 | 4. Which finance/repairs, other expenses, bad debts, prior capped costs and depreciation schedules are evidenced property by property? | Current LF205, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF206 | 5. What construction/land values, acquisition/improvement dates and rental/own-use days support annual and lifetime depreciation and imputation? | Current LF206, exact original unit |
| frozen GitHub-public PR branch body 0b5e62451de50220ac6d80c7813d547d08108641 LF207 | 6. What other annual income, regional minima/credits, foreign tax and prior filings affect final tax and correction? | Current LF207, exact original unit |
